# 开卷考课件整理 Skill — 设计规格

> 面向 Claude Code 的构建说明。目标读者：实现该 skill 的 Claude Code，以及零代码基础的最终用户（大学生）。

---

## 0. 场景与目标

大学生准备**文科开卷考**（如马克思主义基本原理、哲学、法学等）。痛点：课件 PPT 动辄数百页，信噪比低（案例、过场、装饰页冗余）；手动逐章建目录与索引耗时；考场上只能用**纸质打印件**快速翻找、誊抄答案，还需抄画概念图（如数据库 ER 图）。

本 skill 在 Claude Code 中运行，读取用户指定文件夹内的课件，自动产出最多 **6 种可带入考场的 PDF**，并保证每条知识点可溯源到用户手里那份打印件的页码。强调**零代码一键可用**与**绝不臆造**。

---

## 1. 核心设计原则（不可妥协的边界）

1. **抽取与生成严格分离，绝不臆造。** 只重组、压缩、组织源材料，不新增事实、不"补全"知识。任何 AI 生成内容必须物理分开并显式标注「AI 生成，待核」。
2. **全程可溯源。** 每条知识点都挂一个来源锚点，用户能一键回到原文核对/扩展。
3. **内部锚点 = 原始页（真值，永不漂移）。** 锚点 key = `(源文件名, 源内页序号)`；对 .pptx 是"文件名 + 幻灯片序号"，对 .pdf 是"文件名 + PDF 页号"。所有知识点内部都挂在此 key 上。
4. **显示指针按"参考基准"临时换算。** 用户考场带哪份打印件，指针就显示成那份的页码（详见 §4）。L0 页码只是选了 L0 基准时才换算出来。
5. **文科默认保守压缩。** 需要誊抄整段，默认保留完整可抄文本（详见 §5）。
6. **图原样抠取，绝不重绘。** ER 图/概念图等从幻灯片抠出内嵌原图放入产物，重绘会引入错误。
7. **开工前必须先与用户确认**章节结构与所需产物（详见 §7）。

---

## 2. 输入

- 用户只需指明**课件所在文件夹路径**。
- 支持文件类型：`.ppt` / `.pptx` / `.pdf`。
- 以**文字层 PPT 为主**；本期**不做 OCR**（扫描件/无文字层暂不支持，遇到应提示用户而非强行处理）。
- 文件夹内章节命名可能规范、可能缺失、可能混杂——必须靠推断 + 用户确认（§7 Phase 1）。

---

## 3. 六种产物

- 无打印页数上限。
- 用户在开工前勾选需要哪几种（§7 Phase 2）。

---

## 4. 溯源机制（关键）

- **内部真值锚点**：`(源文件名, 源内页序号)`，不可变。所有知识点、QA、索引项都挂此 key。
- **参考基准（reference base）**：Phase 2 的一个设置，决定指针**显示**成什么：
  - 带原 PPT/PDF 打印件 → 显示「源文件名 + 内页序号」（**默认**，尤其未勾 L0 时）。
  - 勾了 L0 且打算打印 L0 → 经 `slide→L0页` 映射显示「L0 p.X」。
- **L0 来源戳（默认开）**：L0 每页角上盖原始来源 key。这样即便用户带错/同时带两份打印件，同一个原始页 key 在原件与 L0 中都能定位，索引永远解析得到。
- 理由：指针必须匹配**考场上物理在手的那份打印件**；用原始页当内部真值则保证清洗规则变化时锚点不漂移。

---

## 5. 精简力度旋钮（文科默认「保守」）

三档，用户可选。skill 应向用户展示如下"同段原文三档对比"示例帮助理解（示例仅演示压缩行为）：

**原文（PPT 节选）**：
> 实践是检验真理的唯一标准。这是因为真理是主观与客观相符合，而判定主观是否符合客观，单靠主观或客观自身都无法完成，只有通过实践这座连接主客观的桥梁才能验证。例如某科学假说是否正确，需经实验反复检验。

- **保守（约原文 80–90%，可直接誊抄）**：删举例，论证链完整保留。
  > 实践是检验真理的唯一标准。原因：真理即主观与客观相符合；而主观是否符合客观，单靠主观或客观自身均无法判定，只有通过实践这座连接主客观的桥梁才能验证。〔源：马原ch5.pptx s.12〕
- **标准（约 40–50%，要点化）**：
  > 实践是检验真理的唯一标准。· 真理＝主观与客观相符合 · 主/客观自身无法自证是否相符 · 实践联结主客观，故能检验〔源：ch5.pptx s.12〕
- **激进（约 15–20%，记忆骨架）**：
  > 实践＝检验真理唯一标准（因联结主客观）〔源：ch5.pptx s.12〕

---

## 6. 工具链（LibreOffice 全自动；零代码一键）

唯一前置（由 doctor/bootstrap 步骤自动检测、缺则引导安装，征求一次同意）：

- **LibreOffice（headless）**：.ppt/.pptx/.pdf → PDF，用于 L0 页空间与转换。
- **Python** + `python-pptx`（抽文字/内嵌图/备注）+ `pypdf`（删页、写书签、盖戳）。
- **Typst（单文件二进制，自动下载）**：渲染 L1/L2/C/M/QA。免 TeX、免编译环境、免管理员权限、原生中文 + 多栏密排。
- **中文字体检测**：doctor 步骤须检查机器是否装有所需中文字体，否则 LibreOffice 转 PDF 会乱码/字体替换。

注意点：
- LibreOffice 对**复杂动画**类幻灯片渲染保真度一般，但静态内容没问题。
- 不使用 pandoc + xelatex（对零代码用户是灾难）。
- 用户全程只需：装好 Claude Code、把课件放进一个文件夹、指明路径。

---

## 7. 开工前交互流程（必须先确认再动手）

---

## 8. 多 Agent 流程与构建顺序

**Token 原则：主控（orchestrator）永不加载原文。** 全程"脚本落盘 → 子 agent 读盘 → 只回简报"，主控只见 manifest 与短报告，上下文有界、子任务可并行可重跑。

构建顺序（**L0/页码映射必须先建**，它定义清洗后页空间与 `slide→L0页` 映射，其余产物都依赖）：

1. **Inventory agent**：分类文件、探文字层、出 manifest。
2. **Chapter-detection agent**：推断章节 → 草稿图 → 用户确认。
3. **Extraction agent（按文件并行）**：LibreOffice 转 PDF 建 L0 页空间；python-pptx 抽文字 + 内嵌图 + 备注；输出归一化 markdown + `(源文件名,源内页序号)→L0页` 映射到 `/work/extracted`。
4. **Distillation agent（按章并行）**：按所选力度产出 L1 条目，每条挂原始页锚点；抽取/生成分离。
5. **Index agent**：关键词 → 锚点，产出 L2。
6. **Mindmap agent**：每章树 + 全局总览。
7. **QA agent**：收集幻灯片中题目+答案；缺答 AI 补并标注。
8. **Render**：Typst 渲染 L1/L2/C/M/QA；pypdf 处理 L0（删废页 + 书签 + 来源戳）。
9. **Coverage/QA-check agent**：每章都有？抽取段落无臆造？低置信处标红待核。

---

## 10. 交给 Claude Code 时请它特别注意

- 先读本规格，**不要跳过 Phase 0–2 的用户确认**直接生成。
- 抽取/生成内容必须可区分；QA 与 L1 里 AI 生成部分一律加「AI 生成，待核」标记。
- 所有页码指针走"原始页内部锚点 + 参考基准换算"，不要把 L0 页码硬编码进知识点。
- 渲染优先 Typst；中文字体先检测。
- 实现时仍需细化：章节推断启发式的阈值、各 Typst 模板的版式（多栏/密度）、L0 废页判定规则（建议可配置 + 可让用户复核删了哪些页）。

## 4. 目前 L1 是怎么抽取/生成的？

**目前完全是确定性流程，没有任何 agent 读取或总结、没有 AI 参与**，分三步：

1. **`extract.py`（机械抽取）**：用 python-pptx 逐张幻灯片把 标题/正文/表格/备注/图片 抠出来，写成 `content.json`。纯机械搬运，一字不改。
2. **`distill_l1.py`（确定性重排）**：读 `content.json`，**一张幻灯片 = 一个条目**，按精简档把正文整段保留（保守档），再加一个**全局流水号**（就是你看到的 `678.xxx` 的来源），算出源指针。也没有总结。
3. **`render.py` + `l1.typ`**：Typst 排版。

所以你看到的"按编号记录"和"扁平"，根源在第 2 步：它只是"每页一条 + 流水号"，没有重建层级。

## 改了什么

**1. 排版 → 竖向·单栏**（[l1.typ](templates/l1.typ)）：A4 竖版、单栏通读，每章另起页，像正常文档/手册阅读。

**2. 层级标题取代流水号**（[distill_l1.py](scripts/distill_l1.py) 重写为"结构保留"）：
- 按 PPT **自身**的「幻灯片标题 + 项目符号缩进层级」机械重建大纲：
  **第N章 → 一、二、节 → 1. 2. 点 → ·明细（按缩进层级递进）**
- 连续同标题的幻灯片**自动合并**成一节（减少碎片）

5. Problem Solving:
   - **`.ppt` vs `.pptx` gap**: python-pptx only reads `.pptx`. Solution: always run LibreOffice to convert `.ppt`→`.pptx` first (cached), then use python-pptx on the converted file
   - **Paragraph indent levels**: PPT `para.level` (0–8) maps naturally to 一、 sections → 1./2. points → · details. The `_build_points()` function handles this mapping
   - **Same-title slide merging**: consecutive slides with identical title in same source file auto-merge in distill_l1.py (anchor list grows, pointer shows range s.N–M)

<!-- role=assistant ts=2026-06-09 21:26 -->

**根本原因**：Typst 对相邻 block 做**间距折叠**（类似 CSS margin collapse）——两个相邻 detail 块之间的实际间距 = `max(above, below)` 而不是相加。当前 detail 的 `above: 0.18em, below: 0.14em`，折叠后只有 `0.18em`，而同段落内换行的行距是 `leading: 0.82em`，两者相差 4.5 倍，所以条目间看起来比行内换行还要挤。

修复：把 detail `above` 提到接近 `leading` 的水平，point 间距同步调整。

<!-- role=assistant ts=2026-06-09 21:47 -->

全部 0 个重复编号。之前验证脚本误报了 `"1.4财产保险的历史考察"`（实际没问题——那是 PPT 小节号 `1.4`，后面跟汉字不是数字，我的修复正则 `\.(?!\d)` 正确地保留了它）。

**修复原理**：

正则 `^\d+(?:[、。]|\.(?!\d))\s*` 只剥离 AI 照搬的**列表序号前缀**：
- `8、大多是…` → 剥除 `8、` ✓
- `9.大多是…` → 剥除 `9.`（后跟汉字，非数字）✓
- `1.4财产保险…` → 保留（`.` 后跟 `4` 是数字）✓
- `2020年…` → 保留（后跟年份）✓

流水线里 merge → apply_screen → render 都已重跑，[outputs/L1.pdf](outputs/L1.pdf) 已更新。

当前 `reference_base = source`，指针显示的是**原始 PPT 页码**，格式为 `源：文件名 s.N`，即清洗前 PPT 的第 N 张幻灯片，与 L0 无关。

两种模式的区别：

如果考场只带 L0.pdf 不带原始 PPT，需要把 `work/config.json` 改成 `"reference_base": "l0"` 再重跑 `merge_ai_l1.py` + `apply_image_screen.py` + `render.py l1`，指针就会自动换算成 L0 页码。

Typst 在内容块 `[...]` 里的多行 `if-else` 会把续行当成普通文本输出。解法：把映射逻辑定义成顶层函数（纯代码块），页脚/页眉里直接调用。同时把页脚标题从 `#meta.title` 换成固定字符串。

<!-- role=assistant ts=2026-06-09 22:12 -->

渲染成功。

**根本原因**：Typst 在内容块 `[...]` 里遇到换行，会把续行的 `else if ...` 当作普通文本输出而不是代码。修复方式是把映射逻辑提到顶层函数（用 `{}` 代码块定义），内容块里只调用函数名——这样无论多少行 `if-else` 都不会泄漏到渲染结果。

同时：
- 页脚标题改为固定字符串 `核心知识手册`
- 抬头说明文字也统一用 `compress-name()` / `refbase-name()` 函数

2. Key Technical Concepts:
   - **Typst margin collapsing**: Adjacent block spacing = `max(above, below)`, not sum — critical for getting consistent spacing between bullet items vs. within-paragraph line spacing
   - **Typst content block limitation**: Multi-line `if-else` inside `[...]` content blocks treats continuation lines as markup text. Fix: define functions at top level using `{...}` code blocks

   - **Typst table rendering**: Parse ` | ` delimited multi-row string → `table()` with alternating row fills

First attempt (replacing curly quotes `"` `"`) failed because these were actually straight ASCII quotes. Fixed with line-by-line repair: for each line starting with `"reason":`, extracted value between outer quotes and applied `re.sub(r'(?<!\\)"', r'\\"', inner)` to escape inner quotes.

   - **Typst multi-line `if-else` in content block rendered as literal text**: After adding `#let compress-label = if ... else if ...` (with continuation lines) inside `[...]`, Typst rendered the `else if` lines as markup text. Shown in user's screenshot — code appeared in PDF header/footer. Fixed by moving to top-level functions using `{...}` code blocks where multi-line control flow works correctly.

   - **Duplicate numbering "8. 8、大多是…"**: AI preserved original PPT list numbers in point text; `merge_ai_l1.py` also added `j.` prefix. Fixed with `_RE_PT_PREFIX = re.compile(r'^\d+(?:[、。]|\.(?!\d))\s*')` applied to point text during merge. The `(?!\d)` lookahead preserves decimal-style section refs like "1.4".

   - **Table rendered as pipe-delimited text**: `render-table()` used `multiline(t)` which just displayed the raw string. Fixed by parsing the string into rows/cells and rendering actual Typst `table()` with grid.

   - **Spacing between adjacent detail items too narrow**: Details `above: 0.18em, below: 0.14em` with margin collapsing → actual gap = 0.18em vs. leading 0.82em. Fixed by increasing to `above: 0.75em, below: 0.5em` → collapsed gap = 0.75em ≈ leading.

2. Key Technical Concepts:

- **Typst**: PDF rendering engine used for L1; top-level functions (`compress-name`, `refbase-name`) required for multi-line if-else logic in content blocks
- **Chapter k-mapping**: chapters.json → prep_ai_distill.py → in_k.json → AI subagents → out_k.json → merge_ai_l1.py → l1.json
- **Margin collapse**: Typst adjacent block spacing = `max(above, below)`, not sum
- **Anchor system**: Internal true-value anchor = `(源文件名, 源内页序号)`, never drifts; display pointer calculated from reference_base
- **Image screening pipeline**: screen_images.py → subagent → image_screen_out.json → apply_image_screen.py
- **Section ID format**: `ch{ci}_sec{si}` (0-based chapter and section indices)
- **AI badge**: `〔AI〕` in red text, not `「AI 生成，待核」`

- **PowerShell here-string with Chinese characters causing git commit failure**: Multi-line `@'...'@` here-strings with Chinese characters caused git to interpret lines as file pathspecs. Fixed by using a simplified `$msg` variable with backtick-n newlines and ASCII-only message.

- **Python f-strings with Chinese in PowerShell one-liners**: Inline Python with Chinese strings in PowerShell caused `SyntaxError: unterminated string literal`. Fixed by writing temporary `.py` files (e.g., `scripts/_merge_out8.py`, `scripts/_check_images.py`) and running them separately.

Summary:
1. Primary Request and Intent:
   This session continued from a previous conversation where AI distillation subagents for chapters k=2-8 had been dispatched and k=1 was already done. k=6 (第9章 机动车辆保险) was reportedly missing.

2. Key Technical Concepts:
   - **Typst margin collapsing**: Adjacent block spacing = `max(above, below)`, not sum — critical for getting consistent spacing between bullet items vs. within-paragraph line spacing
   - **Typst content block limitation**: Multi-line `if-else` inside `[...]` content blocks treats continuation lines as markup text. Fix: define functions at top level using `{...}` code blocks
   - **reference_base**: Controls whether source pointers show original PPT slide numbers (`源：file s.N`) or L0 PDF page numbers (`L0 p.X`). Currently `source`
   - **Image screening pipeline**: `screen_images.py` → subagent reads images + text → `image_screen_out.json` → `apply_image_screen.py` updates l1.json
   - **AI list-number stripping**: Regex `r'^\d+(?:[、。]|\.(?!\d))\s*'` strips AI-reproduced list prefixes while preserving decimal section numbers like "1.4"
   - **Typst table rendering**: Parse ` | ` delimited multi-row string → `table()` with alternating row fills
   - **JSON control character repair**: `\x0b` vertical tab in AI output breaks Python `json.load()`

4. Errors and fixes:

   - **`out_5.json` control character `\x0b`**: `json.load()` raised `JSONDecodeError: Invalid control character at line 160 col 62`. Fixed by reading file text, replacing all characters with `ord(c) < 32` (except \n\r\t) with spaces, re-validating.

   - **`image_screen_out.json` unescaped ASCII double quotes in reason strings**: JSON parse error because AI wrote `"reason": "含"某词"的说明"` with literal `"` inside the string. First attempt (replacing curly quotes `"` `"`) failed because these were actually straight ASCII quotes. Fixed with line-by-line repair: for each line starting with `"reason":`, extracted value between outer quotes and applied `re.sub(r'(?<!\\)"', r'\\"', inner)` to escape inner quotes.

   - **Typst multi-line `if-else` in content block rendered as literal text**: After adding `#let compress-label = if ... else if ...` (with continuation lines) inside `[...]`, Typst rendered the `else if` lines as markup text. Shown in user's screenshot — code appeared in PDF header/footer. Fixed by moving to top-level functions using `{...}` code blocks where multi-line control flow works correctly.

   - **Duplicate numbering "8. 8、大多是…"**: AI preserved original PPT list numbers in point text; `merge_ai_l1.py` also added `j.` prefix. Fixed with `_RE_PT_PREFIX = re.compile(r'^\d+(?:[、。]|\.(?!\d))\s*')` applied to point text during merge. The `(?!\d)` lookahead preserves decimal-style section refs like "1.4".

   - **Table rendered as pipe-delimited text**: `render-table()` used `multiline(t)` which just displayed the raw string. Fixed by parsing the string into rows/cells and rendering actual Typst `table()` with grid.

   - **Spacing between adjacent detail items too narrow**: Details `above: 0.18em, below: 0.14em` with margin collapsing → actual gap = 0.18em vs. leading 0.82em. Fixed by increasing to `above: 0.75em, below: 0.5em` → collapsed gap = 0.75em ≈ leading.

<!-- role=assistant ts=2026-06-09 22:12 -->

渲染成功。

**根本原因**：Typst 在内容块 `[...]` 里遇到换行，会把续行的 `else if ...` 当作普通文本输出而不是代码。修复方式是把映射逻辑提到顶层函数（用 `{}` 代码块定义），内容块里只调用函数名——这样无论多少行 `if-else` 都不会泄漏到渲染结果。

同时：
- 页脚标题改为固定字符串 `核心知识手册`
- 抬头说明文字也统一用 `compress-name()` / `refbase-name()` 函数

从用户视角来看，L2 的完整生成流程分为四个阶段，Claude Code 扮演"主控 + 调度员"的角色：

---

## 前置条件（Phase 0–7 已完成）

Claude 假设用户已经跑通了 L0/L1 管线，即 `work/drafts/chapters.json`（章节结构）和 `work/extracted/*/content.json`（每页结构化文字）已存在。

---

## Claude Code 的实际执行流程

### 第一步：读 SKILL.md，定位 Phase 8

用户说"帮我生成 L2 关键词索引"，Claude Code 加载 `SKILL.md`，找到 Phase 8 节，拿到三条指令：运行 `prep_l2.py` → 按章派子 agent → 运行 `merge_l2.py` → 运行 `render.py l2`。

**SKILL.md 是整个流程的"乐谱"，Claude 是演奏者，不自己发明步骤。**

---

### 第二步：切输入包（确定性，无 AI 介入）

Claude 在终端执行：

脚本读 `chapters.json` + 各章 `content.json`，把每章的幻灯片内容（标题/段落/表格，不含备注）切成 `work/distilled/l2/in_1.json` … `in_7.json`，并写出 `index.json`（记录 k 值、章名、幻灯片数）。

**Claude 只看脚本打印的简报（7行"k=N 章名 N张"），从不把 in_k.json 的全文读进上下文——这是 token 纪律的核心。**

---

### 第三步：并行派子 agent（AI 判断）

Claude 读 `work/distilled/l2/index.json`，得知有 7 章，然后**一次性发出 7 个后台 Task 子 agent**：

每个子 agent **只拥有一章的上下文**——这是避免 7 章内容同时压入主控 context 的关键设计。`agents/keyword.md` 是子 agent 的"岗位说明书"，规定了输出 schema、铁律（禁止 AI 补充定义、锚点必须真实等）。

主控收到简报（如"第6章 49词：高14/中17/低18"），**不读 out_k.json 全文**。

若某个 out_k.json 有 JSON 解析错误（agent 输出了未转义的引号），Claude 运行 `scripts/_fix_l2_json.py` 修复，再继续。

---

### 第四步：合并 + 渲染（确定性）

`merge_l2.py` 做的事情完全是确定性的：读各章 out_k.json，按高→中→低排序，调用 `_pointer()` 生成源指针，写出 `work/distilled/l2.json`。没有任何 AI 判断。

`render.py l2` 调用 Typst，以 `templates/l2.typ` 为模板渲染 `outputs/L2.pdf`。

---

---

2. Key Technical Concepts:
   - **Typst margin collapsing**: Adjacent block spacing = `max(above, below)`, not sum — critical for getting consistent spacing between bullet items vs. within-paragraph line spacing
   - **Typst content block limitation**: Multi-line `if-else` inside `[...]` content blocks treats continuation lines as markup text. Fix: define functions at top level using `{...}` code blocks
   - **reference_base**: Controls whether source pointers show original PPT slide numbers (`源：file s.N`) or L0 PDF page numbers (`L0 p.X`). Currently `source`
   - **Image screening pipeline**: `screen_images.py` → subagent reads images + text → `image_screen_out.json` → `apply_image_screen.py` updates l1.json
   - **AI list-number stripping**: Regex `r'^\d+(?:[、。]|\.(?!\d))\s*'` strips AI-reproduced list prefixes while preserving decimal section numbers like "1.4"
   - **Typst table rendering**: Parse ` | ` delimited multi-row string → `table()` with alternating row fills
   - **JSON control character repair**: `\x0b` vertical tab in AI output breaks Python `json.load()`

3. Files and Code Sections:

   - **`scripts/screen_images.py`** — New script, generates image screening input bundle:

   - **`agents/image_screening.md`** — New agent role definition for image screening:
     - Keep criteria: structural diagrams, data charts, table-as-image
     - Remove criteria: decorative cartoons, text-only slides, title/cover slides
     - Default: keep if uncertain

   - **`templates/TYPOGRAPHY.md`** — New documentation file:

4. Errors and fixes:

   - **`out_5.json` control character `\x0b`**: `json.load()` raised `JSONDecodeError: Invalid control character at line 160 col 62`. Fixed by reading file text, replacing all characters with `ord(c) < 32` (except \n\r\t) with spaces, re-validating.

   - **`image_screen_out.json` unescaped ASCII double quotes in reason strings**: JSON parse error because AI wrote `"reason": "含"某词"的说明"` with literal `"` inside the string. First attempt (replacing curly quotes `"` `"`) failed because these were actually straight ASCII quotes. Fixed with line-by-line repair: for each line starting with `"reason":`, extracted value between outer quotes and applied `re.sub(r'(?<!\\)"', r'\\"', inner)` to escape inner quotes.

   - **Typst multi-line `if-else` in content block rendered as literal text**: After adding `#let compress-label = if ... else if ...` (with continuation lines) inside `[...]`, Typst rendered the `else if` lines as markup text. Shown in user's screenshot — code appeared in PDF header/footer. Fixed by moving to top-level functions using `{...}` code blocks where multi-line control flow works correctly.

   - **Duplicate numbering "8. 8、大多是…"**: AI preserved original PPT list numbers in point text; `merge_ai_l1.py` also added `j.` prefix. Fixed with `_RE_PT_PREFIX = re.compile(r'^\d+(?:[、。]|\.(?!\d))\s*')` applied to point text during merge. The `(?!\d)` lookahead preserves decimal-style section refs like "1.4".

   - **Table rendered as pipe-delimited text**: `render-table()` used `multiline(t)` which just displayed the raw string. Fixed by parsing the string into rows/cells and rendering actual Typst `table()` with grid.

   - **Spacing between adjacent detail items too narrow**: Details `above: 0.18em, below: 0.14em` with margin collapsing → actual gap = 0.18em vs. leading 0.82em. Fixed by increasing to `above: 0.75em, below: 0.5em` → collapsed gap = 0.75em ≈ leading.

渲染成功。

**根本原因**：Typst 在内容块 `[...]` 里遇到换行，会把续行的 `else if ...` 当作普通文本输出而不是代码。修复方式是把映射逻辑提到顶层函数（用 `{}` 代码块定义），内容块里只调用函数名——这样无论多少行 `if-else` 都不会泄漏到渲染结果。

同时：
- 页脚标题改为固定字符串 `核心知识手册`
- 抬头说明文字也统一用 `compress-name()` / `refbase-name()` 函数

   - **definition field**: must be verbatim PPT text; empty string `""` if none — AI补充禁止

     - Key insight: `"` terminates a string only if followed (after whitespace) by `:,}]`
   
   - **Typst render.py UnicodeDecodeError (GBK)**:
     - Cause: `subprocess.run(capture_output=True, text=True)` uses system GBK encoding on Windows; Typst outputs UTF-8 Chinese error messages
     - Behavior: stderr reader thread throws exception, `r.stderr` becomes None; first successful render still returned exit code 0
     - Workaround for direct use: `subprocess.run(cmd, capture_output=True)` (bytes mode) then `.decode('utf-8', errors='replace')`

<!-- role=assistant ts=2026-06-10 15:04 -->

两者定位确实有重叠，但有一个关键区别值得考虑：

**L2** 回答的是"这个词是什么意思"——按章整理、以术语为中心、带频率分级。

**速查卡** 传统上回答"考试时我要查的那条规则/公式/流程是什么"——以**判断/操作**为中心，典型内容是比较表（A险 vs B险的承保范围差异）、条件判断（理赔条件：满足①②③则…）、数字门槛（赔付比例、等待期天数）。

对于保险学这类课程，如果 PPT 里有大量**表格比较**和**条款式列举**，速查卡的价值比 L2 高——因为考场上你查的不是"风险是什么定义"，而是"这种情况下赔不赔、赔多少"。但如果课程内容以概念解释为主、数字很少，L2 + QA 已经覆盖了，速查卡只是换了个排版。

**我的建议**：先看现有 L1.pdf 里是否有大量表格和条件式列举。如果有，速查卡值得做；如果大多是段落式解释，就跳过它，把精力放在 M（思维导图）上——思维导图的章节结构视角与 L1/L2 都不重叠。

<!-- role=user ts=2026-06-10 15:10 -->

确实速查卡还是有必要做的，虽然可能小部分内容与L2重合。思路是类似L1那样先提取PPT中的内容，再用子agent去抽取规则/公式/流程（以判断/操作为中心）等等，最后是合并。渲染pdf的效果复用L1排版配置参考

2. Key Technical Concepts:
   - **Typst margin collapsing**: Adjacent block spacing = `max(above, below)`, not sum — critical for getting consistent spacing between bullet items vs. within-paragraph line spacing
   - **Typst content block limitation**: Multi-line `if-else` inside `[...]` content blocks treats continuation lines as markup text. Fix: define functions at top level using `{...}` code blocks
   - **reference_base**: Controls whether source pointers show original PPT slide numbers (`源：file s.N`) or L0 PDF page numbers (`L0 p.X`). Currently `source`
   - **Image screening pipeline**: `screen_images.py` → subagent reads images + text → `image_screen_out.json` → `apply_image_screen.py` updates l1.json
   - **AI list-number stripping**: Regex `r'^\d+(?:[、。]|\.(?!\d))\s*'` strips AI-reproduced list prefixes while preserving decimal section numbers like "1.4"
   - **Typst table rendering**: Parse ` | ` delimited multi-row string → `table()` with alternating row fills
   - **JSON control character repair**: `\x0b` vertical tab in AI output breaks Python `json.load()`

3. Files and Code Sections:

   - **`templates/l1.typ`** (current final state):

     - Keep criteria: structural diagrams, data charts, table-as-image

4. Errors and fixes:

   - **`out_5.json` control character `\x0b`**: `json.load()` raised `JSONDecodeError: Invalid control character at line 160 col 62`. Fixed by reading file text, replacing all characters with `ord(c) < 32` (except \n\r\t) with spaces, re-validating.

   - **`image_screen_out.json` unescaped ASCII double quotes in reason strings**: JSON parse error because AI wrote `"reason": "含"某词"的说明"` with literal `"` inside the string. First attempt (replacing curly quotes `"` `"`) failed because these were actually straight ASCII quotes. Fixed with line-by-line repair: for each line starting with `"reason":`, extracted value between outer quotes and applied `re.sub(r'(?<!\\)"', r'\\"', inner)` to escape inner quotes.

   - **Typst multi-line `if-else` in content block rendered as literal text**: After adding `#let compress-label = if ... else if ...` (with continuation lines) inside `[...]`, Typst rendered the `else if` lines as markup text. Shown in user's screenshot — code appeared in PDF header/footer. Fixed by moving to top-level functions using `{...}` code blocks where multi-line control flow works correctly.

   - **Duplicate numbering "8. 8、大多是…"**: AI preserved original PPT list numbers in point text; `merge_ai_l1.py` also added `j.` prefix. Fixed with `_RE_PT_PREFIX = re.compile(r'^\d+(?:[、。]|\.(?!\d))\s*')` applied to point text during merge. The `(?!\d)` lookahead preserves decimal-style section refs like "1.4".

   - **Table rendered as pipe-delimited text**: `render-table()` used `multiline(t)` which just displayed the raw string. Fixed by parsing the string into rows/cells and rendering actual Typst `table()` with grid.

   - **Spacing between adjacent detail items too narrow**: Details `above: 0.18em, below: 0.14em` with margin collapsing → actual gap = 0.18em vs. leading 0.82em. Fixed by increasing to `above: 0.75em, below: 0.5em` → collapsed gap = 0.75em ≈ leading.

5. Problem Solving:

   - **Image quality vs. quantity**: 59 images in l1.json were mostly decorative/cartoons. Screening agent kept only 7 meaningful ones (diagrams, charts, policy documents). PDF size halved.
   - **Reference base clarification**: User was uncertain whether "源：…s.N" refers to L0 or original PPT. Confirmed: `reference_base = "source"` → original PPT slide numbers. Switching to `"l0"` would show L0 page numbers instead.
   - **"精简档" label ambiguity**: Footer label "精简档：" looked like it was declaring the document IS in 精简档 mode. Changed to "档位：" with Chinese-name mapping for the value.

<!-- role=assistant ts=2026-06-09 22:12 -->

渲染成功。

**根本原因**：Typst 在内容块 `[...]` 里遇到换行，会把续行的 `else if ...` 当作普通文本输出而不是代码。修复方式是把映射逻辑提到顶层函数（用 `{}` 代码块定义），内容块里只调用函数名——这样无论多少行 `if-else` 都不会泄漏到渲染结果。

同时：
- 页脚标题改为固定字符串 `核心知识手册`
- 抬头说明文字也统一用 `compress-name()` / `refbase-name()` 函数

   - **QA Agent output schema**: `{"chapter_title": "...", "items": [{"type": "简答题|论述题|案例题|其他", "question": "...", "answer": "...", "anchors": ["file#sN"], "ai_answer": bool}]}`
   - **merge_qa.py adds**: `num` field (Q1./Q2./...), `pointer` field (source reference using same `_pointer()` function from distill_l1.py)

4. Errors and fixes:
   - **JSON parse errors in 5 out of 7 agent-produced out_k.json files**:
     - Cause: Chinese text in answer/question strings contained literal unescaped ASCII double quotes (e.g., `"全天有警卫值班"` inside a JSON string value)
     - Files affected: out_1.json, out_2.json, out_4.json, out_5.json, out_7.json
     - Fix: Created `scripts/_fix_qa_json.py` with a state-machine parser. The key insight: a `"` that terminates a JSON string must be followed (after optional whitespace) by `:`, `,`, `}`, or `]`. If not, it's an unescaped quote inside a value and should become `\"`.
     - Result: All 5 files fixed, all 7 files valid JSON afterward
   - **QA.pdf file lock preventing render overwrite**:
     - Cause: Old test QA.pdf (1-chapter, 3-item version) was being held by PDF viewer after being opened for review
     - Fix: Rendered to `QA_new.pdf` instead; user viewed it, said "查看完了"; then deleted old QA.pdf and renamed QA_new.pdf to QA.pdf

   - **Full QA coverage**: Scanned all 7 chapter input packages for question-like slides first; confirmed real content (案例, 例题, 思考题) in all chapters; spawned 7 parallel background agents to process concurrently.
   - **Agent output robustness**: The JSON fixer script was needed because agents inserted raw Chinese quotation marks into JSON strings without escaping. The state-machine approach is more robust than regex since it properly tracks string context.
   - **File lock workaround**: Using alternate filename QA_new.pdf when the canonical output path was locked by PDF viewer, then renaming after user confirmed viewing.

2. Key Technical Concepts:

   - **C pipeline**: `prep_c.py` → per-chapter sub-agents (agents/cheatsheet.md) → `out_k.json` → `merge_c.py` → `c.json` → `render.py c` → `C.pdf`
   - **Four card types**: `formula`（公式/数字门槛）, `rule`（条件判断）, `table`（对比表）, `flow`（流程步骤）
   - **Unified `body` field**: All types use single string field; tables use ` | ` column delimiter and `\n` row delimiter; flows use `①②③\n` step format
   - **Single-column layout**: Unlike L2's two-column, C uses single column (tables need full width)
   - **Type-color semantics**: formula=purple `#7d3c98`, rule=orange `#d35400`, table=blue `#1a5ca8`, flow=green `#1e8449`
   - **Dynamic table parsing in Typst**: `table` type body is parsed from pipe-delimited string into a real Typst table at render time
   - **JSON fixer**: Same state-machine unescaped-quote fixer pattern applied to C outputs (gitignored temp script)
   - **prep_c.py includes `notes`** (unlike prep_l2.py which excluded notes): rules/flows often appear in speaker notes
   - **Typst margin-collapse**: Same `leading: 0.75em` base; `block(below: 0.5em)` for title-to-content spacing
   - **TYPE_ORDER sort**: `{"formula": 0, "rule": 1, "table": 2, "flow": 3}` — most specific/testable content first
   - **GBK encoding warning on Windows**: Known non-fatal issue with subprocess stderr reader; render succeeds despite warning

3. Files and Code Sections:

   - **`scripts/prep_c.py`** (created):
     - Same structure as prep_l2.py but includes `notes` field (rules/flows often in speaker notes)
     - Outputs `work/distilled/c/in_<k>.json` + `c/index.json`
     

   - **`agents/cheatsheet.md`** (created):
     - Sub-agent role for C cheatsheet extraction per chapter
     - Reads `work/distilled/c/in_<k>.json`, writes `out_<k>.json`
     - Output schema:
     

     - Iron rules: body from PPT verbatim only; table columns MUST use ` | ` (space-pipe-space); no AI fabrication; pure definitions not collected; 3-15 cards per chapter
     - Returns brief report only (type counts), not full JSON

   - **`scripts/merge_c.py`** (created):
     - Reads all `out_k.json`, sorts by TYPE_ORDER then label
     - Adds `pointer` via `_pointer()` from `distill_l1.py`

4. Errors and Fixes:

   - **JSON parse errors in 3/7 C agent outputs** (out_2, out_3, out_6):
     - Cause: Chinese body text contained unescaped ASCII double quotes inside JSON string values
     - Fix: Created `scripts/_fix_c_json.py` reusing identical state-machine logic from `_fix_l2_json.py`
     - State machine: `"` terminates a string only if followed (after optional whitespace) by `:,}]`
     - Result: out_2 fixed to 16 cards, out_3 to 21 cards, out_6 to 25 cards

   - **GBK encoding UnicodeDecodeError on render**:
     - Same known issue as L2: `subprocess.run(capture_output=True, text=True)` uses system GBK encoding on Windows, Typst outputs UTF-8 Chinese error messages to stderr
     - Non-fatal: render still returns exit code 0 and produces valid PDF
     - No fix needed (workaround documented in prior session)

5. Problem Solving:

   - **L2 vs C distinction**: Key insight is orientation — L2 is definition-centric ("what is this term"), C is actionable-centric ("what condition/rule/process do I need during exam"). For insurance courses with many comparison tables and conditional clauses, both serve distinct purposes.
   - **Single vs double column**: Chose single column for C because tables need full width; L2 chose double column because term cards are narrow. This was a conscious layout choice.
   - **Unified body field**: Instead of type-specific fields (steps[], headers[], rows[]), used a single `body` string with format conventions. Simpler agent schema, simpler merge script; only the Typst template needs type-aware rendering.
   - **Dynamic table parsing in Typst**: Pipe-delimited string parsed at render time using `split(" | ")` and `split("\n")`. Works with Typst's `.trim()` method (available in Typst 0.11+).
   - **notes inclusion in prep_c.py**: Deliberate decision to include speaker notes (unlike prep_l2.py) because rules and flow conditions often appear in presentation notes rather than visible slide content.

2. Key Technical Concepts:
   - **Typst margin collapsing**: Adjacent block spacing = `max(above, below)`, not sum — critical for getting consistent spacing between bullet items vs. within-paragraph line spacing
   - **Typst content block limitation**: Multi-line `if-else` inside `[...]` content blocks treats continuation lines as markup text. Fix: define functions at top level using `{...}` code blocks
   - **reference_base**: Controls whether source pointers show original PPT slide numbers (`源：file s.N`) or L0 PDF page numbers (`L0 p.X`). Currently `source`
   - **Image screening pipeline**: `screen_images.py` → subagent reads images + text → `image_screen_out.json` → `apply_image_screen.py` updates l1.json
   - **AI list-number stripping**: Regex `r'^\d+(?:[、。]|\.(?!\d))\s*'` strips AI-reproduced list prefixes while preserving decimal section numbers like "1.4"
   - **Typst table rendering**: Parse ` | ` delimited multi-row string → `table()` with alternating row fills
   - **JSON control character repair**: `\x0b` vertical tab in AI output breaks Python `json.load()`

3. Files and Code Sections:

4. Errors and fixes:

   - **`out_5.json` control character `\x0b`**: `json.load()` raised `JSONDecodeError: Invalid control character at line 160 col 62`. Fixed by reading file text, replacing all characters with `ord(c) < 32` (except \n\r\t) with spaces, re-validating.

   - **`image_screen_out.json` unescaped ASCII double quotes in reason strings**: JSON parse error because AI wrote `"reason": "含"某词"的说明"` with literal `"` inside the string. First attempt (replacing curly quotes `"` `"`) failed because these were actually straight ASCII quotes. Fixed with line-by-line repair: for each line starting with `"reason":`, extracted value between outer quotes and applied `re.sub(r'(?<!\\)"', r'\\"', inner)` to escape inner quotes.

   - **Typst multi-line `if-else` in content block rendered as literal text**: After adding `#let compress-label = if ... else if ...` (with continuation lines) inside `[...]`, Typst rendered the `else if` lines as markup text. Shown in user's screenshot — code appeared in PDF header/footer. Fixed by moving to top-level functions using `{...}` code blocks where multi-line control flow works correctly.

   - **Duplicate numbering "8. 8、大多是…"**: AI preserved original PPT list numbers in point text; `merge_ai_l1.py` also added `j.` prefix. Fixed with `_RE_PT_PREFIX = re.compile(r'^\d+(?:[、。]|\.(?!\d))\s*')` applied to point text during merge. The `(?!\d)` lookahead preserves decimal-style section refs like "1.4".

   - **Table rendered as pipe-delimited text**: `render-table()` used `multiline(t)` which just displayed the raw string. Fixed by parsing the string into rows/cells and rendering actual Typst `table()` with grid.

   - **Spacing between adjacent detail items too narrow**: Details `above: 0.18em, below: 0.14em` with margin collapsing → actual gap = 0.18em vs. leading 0.82em. Fixed by increasing to `above: 0.75em, below: 0.5em` → collapsed gap = 0.75em ≈ leading.

5. Problem Solving:

   - **Image quality vs. quantity**: 59 images in l1.json were mostly decorative/cartoons. Screening agent kept only 7 meaningful ones (diagrams, charts, policy documents). PDF size halved.
   - **Reference base clarification**: User was uncertain whether "源：…s.N" refers to L0 or original PPT. Confirmed: `reference_base = "source"` → original PPT slide numbers. Switching to `"l0"` would show L0 page numbers instead.

<!-- role=assistant ts=2026-06-09 22:12 -->

渲染成功。

**根本原因**：Typst 在内容块 `[...]` 里遇到换行，会把续行的 `else if ...` 当作普通文本输出而不是代码。修复方式是把映射逻辑提到顶层函数（用 `{}` 代码块定义），内容块里只调用函数名——这样无论多少行 `if-else` 都不会泄漏到渲染结果。

同时：
- 页脚标题改为固定字符串 `核心知识手册`
- 抬头说明文字也统一用 `compress-name()` / `refbase-name()` 函数

4. Errors and fixes:

Fixed by using ToolSearch to load the schema first, then properly including the full JSON questions array in the parameter.

- **PowerShell here-string with Chinese characters causing git commit failure**: Multi-line `@'...'@` here-strings with Chinese characters caused git to interpret lines as file pathspecs. Fixed by using a simplified `$msg` variable with backtick-n newlines and ASCII-only message.

- **Python f-strings with Chinese in PowerShell one-liners**: Inline Python with Chinese strings in PowerShell caused `SyntaxError: unterminated string literal`. Fixed by writing temporary `.py` files (e.g., `scripts/_merge_out8.py`, `scripts/_check_images.py`) and running them separately.

- **UnicodeDecodeError in render.py**: `'gbk' codec can't decode byte 0x80` in subprocess output reading thread. Not a real render failure — render.py completed successfully, this was only a stderr output-reading issue. No fix needed.

     - Fix: Created `scripts/_fix_qa_json.py` with a state-machine parser. The key insight: a `"` that terminates a JSON string must be followed (after optional whitespace) by `:`, `,`, `}`, or `]`. If not, it's an unescaped quote inside a value and should become `\"`.
     - Result: All 5 files fixed, all 7 files valid JSON afterward
   - **QA.pdf file lock preventing render overwrite**:
     - Cause: Old test QA.pdf (1-chapter, 3-item version) was being held by PDF viewer after being opened for review
     - Fix: Rendered to `QA_new.pdf` instead; user viewed it, said "查看完了"; then deleted old QA.pdf and renamed QA_new.pdf to QA.pdf

从用户视角来看，L2 的完整生成流程分为四个阶段，Claude Code 扮演"主控 + 调度员"的角色：

---

## 前置条件（Phase 0–7 已完成）

Claude 假设用户已经跑通了 L0/L1 管线，即 `work/drafts/chapters.json`（章节结构）和 `work/extracted/*/content.json`（每页结构化文字）已存在。

---

## Claude Code 的实际执行流程

### 第一步：读 SKILL.md，定位 Phase 8

用户说"帮我生成 L2 关键词索引"，Claude Code 加载 `SKILL.md`，找到 Phase 8 节，拿到三条指令：运行 `prep_l2.py` → 按章派子 agent → 运行 `merge_l2.py` → 运行 `render.py l2`。

**SKILL.md 是整个流程的"乐谱"，Claude 是演奏者，不自己发明步骤。**

---

### 第二步：切输入包（确定性，无 AI 介入）

Claude 在终端执行：

脚本读 `chapters.json` + 各章 `content.json`，把每章的幻灯片内容（标题/段落/表格，不含备注）切成 `work/distilled/l2/in_1.json` … `in_7.json`，并写出 `index.json`（记录 k 值、章名、幻灯片数）。

**Claude 只看脚本打印的简报（7行"k=N 章名 N张"），从不把 in_k.json 的全文读进上下文——这是 token 纪律的核心。**

---

### 第三步：并行派子 agent（AI 判断）

Claude 读 `work/distilled/l2/index.json`，得知有 7 章，然后**一次性发出 7 个后台 Task 子 agent**：

每个子 agent **只拥有一章的上下文**——这是避免 7 章内容同时压入主控 context 的关键设计。`agents/keyword.md` 是子 agent 的"岗位说明书"，规定了输出 schema、铁律（禁止 AI 补充定义、锚点必须真实等）。

主控收到简报（如"第6章 49词：高14/中17/低18"），**不读 out_k.json 全文**。

若某个 out_k.json 有 JSON 解析错误（agent 输出了未转义的引号），Claude 运行 `scripts/_fix_l2_json.py` 修复，再继续。

---

### 第四步：合并 + 渲染（确定性）

`merge_l2.py` 做的事情完全是确定性的：读各章 out_k.json，按高→中→低排序，调用 `_pointer()` 生成源指针，写出 `work/distilled/l2.json`。没有任何 AI 判断。

`render.py l2` 调用 Typst，以 `templates/l2.typ` 为模板渲染 `outputs/L2.pdf`。

---

## 文件依赖关系一览

---

## 设计取舍总结

Summary:
1. Primary Request and Intent:

   **Session context**: This session continued from a prior conversation where QA.pdf (7 chapters, 56 questions) was completed and README was updated. The current session had two requests:

   1. **Build L2 关键词索引 PDF pipeline**: "接着，完成L2 关键词索引 pdf的产出，思路是类似L1那样先提取PPT中的内容，再用子agent去抽取高/中/低频关键词或术语等等，最后是合并。渲染pdf的效果复用L1排版配置参考"
   2. **Fix spacing and document typography**: User observed粗体关键词与其下方内容的间距过小 and requested "这样的调整修改渲染配置需要记录，以便开源后的用户使用时也能获得一样的效果"
   3. **Workflow explanation question (most recent)**: "从用户视角看的话，如果完整使用了这个项目，要生成L2.pdf,那么claude code的工作流程是怎样的（具体是会参考与使用哪些文件，会怎样安排子agent等等）？"

2. Key Technical Concepts:

   - **L2 Pipeline**: `prep_l2.py` → per-chapter subagents (agents/keyword.md) → `out_k.json` → `merge_l2.py` → `l2.json` → `render.py l2` → `L2.pdf`
   - **Sub-agent schema**: `{"chapter_title": "...", "terms": [{"term": "...", "freq": "高|中|低", "definition": "...", "anchors": ["file#sN"]}]}`
   - **Freq levels**: 高=核心概念(red #c0392b) / 中=重要术语(blue #1a5ca8) / 低=一般词汇(gray #888888)
   - **definition field**: must be verbatim PPT text; empty string `""` if none — AI补充禁止
   - **Typst two-column layout**: `columns(2, gutter: 1.2em)` per chapter; chapter navy headers outside columns block (full-width)
   - **term-block**: left colored border + freq badge + bold term + definition + source pointer
   - **JSON fixer state-machine**: same unescaped double-quote issue as QA agents; same `fix_json_unescaped_quotes()` logic applied to L2 files
   - **Typst margin-collapse**: `max(above, below)` determines actual spacing; `below: 0.6em ≈ leading: 0.75em` prevents term-to-definition visual sticking

4. Errors and Fixes:

   - **JSON parse errors in 3/7 L2 agent outputs** (out_1, out_2, out_6):
     - Cause: Chinese definition text contained unescaped ASCII double quotes inside JSON string values
     - Fix: Created `scripts/_fix_l2_json.py` reusing same state-machine logic as `_fix_qa_json.py`
     - Key insight: `"` terminates a string only if followed (after whitespace) by `:,}]`
   
   - **Typst render.py UnicodeDecodeError (GBK)**:
     - Cause: `subprocess.run(capture_output=True, text=True)` uses system GBK encoding on Windows; Typst outputs UTF-8 Chinese error messages
     - Behavior: stderr reader thread throws exception, `r.stderr` becomes None; first successful render still returned exit code 0
     - Workaround for direct use: `subprocess.run(cmd, capture_output=True)` (bytes mode) then `.decode('utf-8', errors='replace')`

   - **PDF file lock preventing render overwrite**:
     - Cause: L2.pdf held open by PDF viewer after first render
     - Fix: Rendered to `L2_new.pdf`, user confirmed viewing, then deleted old + renamed

   - **Term-to-definition spacing too tight** (user feedback):
     - Cause: `block(below: 0.3em)` on term header was far below `leading: 0.75em`
     - Fix: `below: 0.3em` → `below: 0.6em`; `inset top/bottom: 3/4pt` → `5/5pt`
     - Documented in TYPOGRAPHY.md with debugging table

   - **PowerShell inline Python script backslash escaping failure**:
     - When trying to run state-machine fix as a one-liner in bash, backslash escaping of `\\` caused SyntaxError
     - Fix: wrote a proper `.py` script file and ran it instead

5. Problem Solving:

   - **Pipeline design**: Chose chapter-based (not global alphabetical) organization because sub-agents work per-chapter, and per-chapter grouping serves study flow (students know what chapter a topic is in). No cross-chapter deduplication for simplicity.
   - **Definition sourcing**: Strict rule that `definition` comes from PPT verbatim or is empty—prevents AI hallucination of definitions that could mislead in open-book exams.
   - **Two-column Typst layout**: Used `columns(2, gutter: 1.2em)` content function per chapter, with chapter headers placed before the `columns()` call to ensure they span full width.
   - **Typography spacing derivation**: Applied same margin-collapse principle from L1: block.below ≥ leading to prevent visual sticking between term and definition.

<!-- role=assistant ts=2026-06-10 15:04 -->

两者定位确实有重叠，但有一个关键区别值得考虑：

**L2** 回答的是"这个词是什么意思"——按章整理、以术语为中心、带频率分级。

**速查卡** 传统上回答"考试时我要查的那条规则/公式/流程是什么"——以**判断/操作**为中心，典型内容是比较表（A险 vs B险的承保范围差异）、条件判断（理赔条件：满足①②③则…）、数字门槛（赔付比例、等待期天数）。

对于保险学这类课程，如果 PPT 里有大量**表格比较**和**条款式列举**，速查卡的价值比 L2 高——因为考场上你查的不是"风险是什么定义"，而是"这种情况下赔不赔、赔多少"。但如果课程内容以概念解释为主、数字很少，L2 + QA 已经覆盖了，速查卡只是换了个排版。

**我的建议**：先看现有 L1.pdf 里是否有大量表格和条件式列举。如果有，速查卡值得做；如果大多是段落式解释，就跳过它，把精力放在 M（思维导图）上——思维导图的章节结构视角与 L1/L2 都不重叠。

     - Fix: Added `isinstance(tbl, str)` check before dict access

   - **JSON parse errors in out_2.json and out_6.json**
     - Cause: Chinese body text contained unescaped ASCII double-quote characters inside JSON string values
     - Fix: Created `scripts/_fix_m_json.py` using identical state-machine logic from `_fix_c_json.py`
       - `"` terminates a string only if followed (after optional whitespace) by `:,}]`
     - Result: out_2 fixed (10 branches), out_6 fixed (10 branches)

   - **M.pdf was card-grid, not tree-style mindmap (user feedback)**
     - User explicitly rejected Typst card-grid output
     - Fix: Completely replaced Typst renderer with matplotlib tree renderer (`render_m.py`)
     - New approach: A4 landscape, root left, elbow connectors, branches middle, nodes right

   - **Node text too long / tree too dense**
     - Cause: Agents output 17-41 character nodes; dense chapters (e.g., ch1: 64 nodes) had slot_h=0.13 inches
     - Fix: Added adaptive `MIN_SLOT_H=0.21` — if total_nodes × 0.21 > AVAIL_H, reduce nodes per branch proportionally (min 2), show "+N" indicator for truncated nodes
     - Dynamic font: 8pt for spacious pages, 7pt for dense pages

   - **Root node title breaks awkwardly (e.g., "第14章 责" + newline + "任保险")**
     - Fix: Added `split_chapter_title()` function that splits at "章" boundary
     - Result: "第14章\n责任保险" instead of mid-word break

5. Problem Solving:

   - **How to render actual tree-style mindmap in Python:** Researched options (graphviz, matplotlib, reportlab, CeTZ/Fletcher in Typst). Chose matplotlib because: Anaconda already has it, Microsoft YaHei font available, `PdfPages` handles multi-page PDF, manual layout gives full control
   - **Slot-based tree layout algorithm:** Total slots = sum(node counts per branch); each slot gets AVAIL_H/total_slots inches; branch center = average of its allocated slots; leaf node centers = evenly distributed within branch's slots
   - **Adaptive density:** MIN_SLOT_H=0.21 inches caps density; ratio = max_total/total_raw reduces node counts proportionally; "+N" suffix informs user of truncated content
   - **CJK in matplotlib:** `plt.rcParams["font.sans-serif"] = ["Microsoft YaHei", "SimHei", "DejaVu Sans"]` — verified working with test PDF

看了代码，找到了两个问题的根因。

---

## 问题 1：全局总览分支文字截断

[scripts/render_m.py:184](scripts/render_m.py#L184) 有一个硬编码上限：

agent 产出的分支标题是 6–12 字，9 字上限直接砍掉了很多正常长度的标题。这是代码写死的，没有动态原因。

---

## 问题 2：逐章树形 — 截断 + "+N" + 右半页空白

**这三个症状来自同一个根因：水平空间没被用上。**

看布局常量（L：[render_m.py:204-211](scripts/render_m.py#L204)）：

叶节点文字从 3.86 英寸开始，可用宽度 = 11.69 − 3.86 − 0.22（右边距）= **7.6 英寸**。

但当前 `max_chars` 只有 20–24 字符（[render_m.py:237-241](scripts/render_m.py#L237)），以 8pt 字体估算 20 字约占 1.8 英寸——右侧 5.8 英寸完全空白。

**这才是"半页空白"的真正来源**，不是垂直方向的问题，是文字只占了水平可用宽度的 24%。

然后 `MIN_SLOT_H = 0.21` 这个垂直约束（[render_m.py:217](scripts/render_m.py#L217)）是为了"文字不能太密"而加的——但它导致节点被等比例砍掉，并加 "+N"。这个约束的前提假设（每节点只能有 20 字）已经不成立了，因为节点可以用更多字写在右侧。

---

## 优化思路

### 方向一：彻底用满水平空间

- `max_chars` 从 20–24 提高到 40–50 字（约 3.6–4.5 英寸，在可用的 7.6 英寸内仍有余量）
- 如果某节点超过单行上限，支持 2 行换行（`wrap_cn`），每节点占 2 个槽高
- 移除 MIN_SLOT_H 强制减少节点数的逻辑，或将阈值降到 0.13 英寸（6.5pt 可读的最小行高）
- 取消 "+N" 指示（既然目标是"考场参考"，截断本身就失去了意义）

**权衡**：对第 1 章（64 个节点）：slot_h = 7.71 / 64 = 0.12 英寸，在 6–6.5pt 字体下可读但比较密。对 40 个节点以内的章节：0.19 英寸以上，轻松显示完整内容。

### 方向二：更保守的改动（只修 bug，不改布局）

- 把 `max_chars` 提高到 35 字（约 3.2 英寸，保守但比现在翻倍）
- 把 MIN_SLOT_H 降到 0.15 英寸，让更多节点得以显示
- "+N" 改为只在真正空间不足时才出现

---

## 我的建议

采用**方向一**，理由是：
1. 右半页空白本来就是浪费——节点文字长一倍，可读性反而更好
2. 取消 "+N" 对考场实用性提升是实质性的
3. 对最密的章节（64 节点），可以接受 6pt 的紧凑字体

2. Key Technical Concepts:
   - **Typst margin collapsing**: Adjacent block spacing = `max(above, below)`, not sum — critical for getting consistent spacing between bullet items vs. within-paragraph line spacing
   - **Typst content block limitation**: Multi-line `if-else` inside `[...]` content blocks treats continuation lines as markup text. Fix: define functions at top level using `{...}` code blocks
   - **reference_base**: Controls whether source pointers show original PPT slide numbers (`源：file s.N`) or L0 PDF page numbers (`L0 p.X`). Currently `source`
   - **Image screening pipeline**: `screen_images.py` → subagent reads images + text → `image_screen_out.json` → `apply_image_screen.py` updates l1.json
   - **AI list-number stripping**: Regex `r'^\d+(?:[、。]|\.(?!\d))\s*'` strips AI-reproduced list prefixes while preserving decimal section numbers like "1.4"
   - **Typst table rendering**: Parse ` | ` delimited multi-row string → `table()` with alternating row fills
   - **JSON control character repair**: `\x0b` vertical tab in AI output breaks Python `json.load()`

3. Files and Code Sections:

   - **`templates/l1.typ`** (current final state):
     

     
     Table rendering (key fix):
     

     
     Spacing (margin-collapse aware):
     

     
     AI badge:
     

   - **`scripts/merge_ai_l1.py`** — Added `_RE_PT_PREFIX` to strip AI-reproduced list numbers:
     

   - **`scripts/screen_images.py`** — New script, generates image screening input bundle:
     - Reads l1.json, collects all sections with images
     - Outputs `work/distilled/ai/image_screen_in.json` with sec_id, chapter_title, heading, text_summary (600 chars), images list

   - **`scripts/apply_image_screen.py`** — New script, applies screening decisions:
     - Reads `image_screen_out.json`, builds `{sec_id: {img_path: keep}}` index
     - Filters l1.json images lists, writes back
     - Reports: kept 7, removed 52 out of 59

   - **`scripts/verify_l1.py`** — New quality verification script:
     - Checks all sections have source pointers
     - Checks all anchors parseable, content.json exists
     - Prints per-chapter first-section preview

   - **`agents/image_screening.md`** — New agent role definition for image screening:
     - Keep criteria: structural diagrams, data charts, table-as-image
     - Remove criteria: decorative cartoons, text-only slides, title/cover slides
     - Default: keep if uncertain

   - **`templates/TYPOGRAPHY.md`** — New documentation file:
     - Records all spacing parameters with reasoning
     - Documents margin-collapse mechanism and its implications

     - 8 AI-distilled chapters, 201 sections

   - **`outputs/L1.pdf`** — Final rendered product:

4. Errors and fixes:

   - **`out_5.json` control character `\x0b`**: `json.load()` raised `JSONDecodeError: Invalid control character at line 160 col 62`. Fixed by reading file text, replacing all characters with `ord(c) < 32` (except \n\r\t) with spaces, re-validating.

   - **`image_screen_out.json` unescaped ASCII double quotes in reason strings**: JSON parse error because AI wrote `"reason": "含"某词"的说明"` with literal `"` inside the string. First attempt (replacing curly quotes `"` `"`) failed because these were actually straight ASCII quotes. Fixed with line-by-line repair: for each line starting with `"reason":`, extracted value between outer quotes and applied `re.sub(r'(?<!\\)"', r'\\"', inner)` to escape inner quotes.

   - **Typst multi-line `if-else` in content block rendered as literal text**: After adding `#let compress-label = if ... else if ...` (with continuation lines) inside `[...]`, Typst rendered the `else if` lines as markup text. Shown in user's screenshot — code appeared in PDF header/footer. Fixed by moving to top-level functions using `{...}` code blocks where multi-line control flow works correctly.

   - **Duplicate numbering "8. 8、大多是…"**: AI preserved original PPT list numbers in point text; `merge_ai_l1.py` also added `j.` prefix. Fixed with `_RE_PT_PREFIX = re.compile(r'^\d+(?:[、。]|\.(?!\d))\s*')` applied to point text during merge. The `(?!\d)` lookahead preserves decimal-style section refs like "1.4".

   - **Table rendered as pipe-delimited text**: `render-table()` used `multiline(t)` which just displayed the raw string. Fixed by parsing the string into rows/cells and rendering actual Typst `table()` with grid.

   - **Spacing between adjacent detail items too narrow**: Details `above: 0.18em, below: 0.14em` with margin collapsing → actual gap = 0.18em vs. leading 0.82em. Fixed by increasing to `above: 0.75em, below: 0.5em` → collapsed gap = 0.75em ≈ leading.

5. Problem Solving:

   - **Image quality vs. quantity**: 59 images in l1.json were mostly decorative/cartoons. Screening agent kept only 7 meaningful ones (diagrams, charts, policy documents). PDF size halved.

<!-- role=assistant ts=2026-06-09 22:12 -->

渲染成功。

**根本原因**：Typst 在内容块 `[...]` 里遇到换行，会把续行的 `else if ...` 当作普通文本输出而不是代码。修复方式是把映射逻辑提到顶层函数（用 `{}` 代码块定义），内容块里只调用函数名——这样无论多少行 `if-else` 都不会泄漏到渲染结果。

同时：
- 页脚标题改为固定字符串 `核心知识手册`
- 抬头说明文字也统一用 `compress-name()` / `refbase-name()` 函数

   - **merge_qa.py adds**: `num` field (Q1./Q2./...), `pointer` field (source reference using same `_pointer()` function from distill_l1.py)

4. Errors and fixes:
   - **JSON parse errors in 5 out of 7 agent-produced out_k.json files**:
     - Cause: Chinese text in answer/question strings contained literal unescaped ASCII double quotes (e.g., `"全天有警卫值班"` inside a JSON string value)
     - Files affected: out_1.json, out_2.json, out_4.json, out_5.json, out_7.json
     - Fix: Created `scripts/_fix_qa_json.py` with a state-machine parser. The key insight: a `"` that terminates a JSON string must be followed (after optional whitespace) by `:`, `,`, `}`, or `]`. If not, it's an unescaped quote inside a value and should become `\"`.
     - Result: All 5 files fixed, all 7 files valid JSON afterward
   - **QA.pdf file lock preventing render overwrite**:

从用户视角来看，L2 的完整生成流程分为四个阶段，Claude Code 扮演"主控 + 调度员"的角色：

---

## 前置条件（Phase 0–7 已完成）

Claude 假设用户已经跑通了 L0/L1 管线，即 `work/drafts/chapters.json`（章节结构）和 `work/extracted/*/content.json`（每页结构化文字）已存在。

---

## Claude Code 的实际执行流程

### 第一步：读 SKILL.md，定位 Phase 8

用户说"帮我生成 L2 关键词索引"，Claude Code 加载 `SKILL.md`，找到 Phase 8 节，拿到三条指令：运行 `prep_l2.py` → 按章派子 agent → 运行 `merge_l2.py` → 运行 `render.py l2`。

**SKILL.md 是整个流程的"乐谱"，Claude 是演奏者，不自己发明步骤。**

---

### 第二步：切输入包（确定性，无 AI 介入）

Claude 在终端执行：

脚本读 `chapters.json` + 各章 `content.json`，把每章的幻灯片内容（标题/段落/表格，不含备注）切成 `work/distilled/l2/in_1.json` … `in_7.json`，并写出 `index.json`（记录 k 值、章名、幻灯片数）。

**Claude 只看脚本打印的简报（7行"k=N 章名 N张"），从不把 in_k.json 的全文读进上下文——这是 token 纪律的核心。**

---

### 第三步：并行派子 agent（AI 判断）

Claude 读 `work/distilled/l2/index.json`，得知有 7 章，然后**一次性发出 7 个后台 Task 子 agent**：

每个子 agent **只拥有一章的上下文**——这是避免 7 章内容同时压入主控 context 的关键设计。`agents/keyword.md` 是子 agent 的"岗位说明书"，规定了输出 schema、铁律（禁止 AI 补充定义、锚点必须真实等）。

主控收到简报（如"第6章 49词：高14/中17/低18"），**不读 out_k.json 全文**。

若某个 out_k.json 有 JSON 解析错误（agent 输出了未转义的引号），Claude 运行 `scripts/_fix_l2_json.py` 修复，再继续。

---

### 第四步：合并 + 渲染（确定性）

`merge_l2.py` 做的事情完全是确定性的：读各章 out_k.json，按高→中→低排序，调用 `_pointer()` 生成源指针，写出 `work/distilled/l2.json`。没有任何 AI 判断。

`render.py l2` 调用 Typst，以 `templates/l2.typ` 为模板渲染 `outputs/L2.pdf`。

---

---

<!-- role=assistant ts=2026-06-10 15:04 -->

两者定位确实有重叠，但有一个关键区别值得考虑：

**L2** 回答的是"这个词是什么意思"——按章整理、以术语为中心、带频率分级。

**速查卡** 传统上回答"考试时我要查的那条规则/公式/流程是什么"——以**判断/操作**为中心，典型内容是比较表（A险 vs B险的承保范围差异）、条件判断（理赔条件：满足①②③则…）、数字门槛（赔付比例、等待期天数）。

对于保险学这类课程，如果 PPT 里有大量**表格比较**和**条款式列举**，速查卡的价值比 L2 高——因为考场上你查的不是"风险是什么定义"，而是"这种情况下赔不赔、赔多少"。但如果课程内容以概念解释为主、数字很少，L2 + QA 已经覆盖了，速查卡只是换了个排版。

**我的建议**：先看现有 L1.pdf 里是否有大量表格和条件式列举。如果有，速查卡值得做；如果大多是段落式解释，就跳过它，把精力放在 M（思维导图）上——思维导图的章节结构视角与 L1/L2 都不重叠。

   - **Typst margin-collapse**: Same `leading: 0.75em` base; `block(below: 0.5em)` for title-to-content spacing
   - **TYPE_ORDER sort**: `{"formula": 0, "rule": 1, "table": 2, "flow": 3}` — most specific/testable content first

4. Errors and Fixes:

   - **JSON parse errors in 3/7 C agent outputs** (out_2, out_3, out_6):
     - Cause: Chinese body text contained unescaped ASCII double quotes inside JSON string values
     - Fix: Created `scripts/_fix_c_json.py` reusing identical state-machine logic from `_fix_l2_json.py`
     - State machine: `"` terminates a string only if followed (after optional whitespace) by `:,}]`
     - Result: out_2 fixed to 16 cards, out_3 to 21 cards, out_6 to 25 cards

   - **GBK encoding UnicodeDecodeError on render**:
     - Same known issue as L2: `subprocess.run(capture_output=True, text=True)` uses system GBK encoding on Windows, Typst outputs UTF-8 Chinese error messages to stderr
     - Non-fatal: render still returns exit code 0 and produces valid PDF
     - No fix needed (workaround documented in prior session)

5. Problem Solving:

   - **L2 vs C distinction**: Key insight is orientation — L2 is definition-centric ("what is this term"), C is actionable-centric ("what condition/rule/process do I need during exam"). For insurance courses with many comparison tables and conditional clauses, both serve distinct purposes.
   - **Single vs double column**: Chose single column for C because tables need full width; L2 chose double column because term cards are narrow. This was a conscious layout choice.
   - **Unified body field**: Instead of type-specific fields (steps[], headers[], rows[]), used a single `body` string with format conventions. Simpler agent schema, simpler merge script; only the Typst template needs type-aware rendering.
   - **Dynamic table parsing in Typst**: Pipe-delimited string parsed at render time using `split(" | ")` and `split("\n")`. Works with Typst's `.trim()` method (available in Typst 0.11+).
   - **notes inclusion in prep_c.py**: Deliberate decision to include speaker notes (unlike prep_l2.py) because rules and flow conditions often appear in presentation notes rather than visible slide content.

   - **Adaptive density**: MIN_SLOT_H=0.115 (down from 0.21); downgrade 2→1 line first, then proportional node reduction as last resort

joined = "\n".join(installed)

Windows 注册表值形如 "Microsoft YaHei & Microsoft YaHei UI (TrueType)"，"Microsoft YaHei" 会正确匹配。但 "SimHei" 也会匹配 "SimHeiBold (TrueType)"（若存在）等衍生条目，不影响功能但语义不精确。

更重要的缺陷：注册表值名是 "字体名 (TrueType)" 形式，而 Typst 需要的是字体族名。doctor 通过不代表 Typst 一定找得到同名字体族。

递归扫描无文件数量上限和时间限制。用户若不小心给了 C:\ 或 /home，会触发遍历成千上万个目录，且过滤后才统计文件数，中途没有进度提示。

【S8】l1.typ first 变量在 Typst 中的作用域
文件：templates/l1.typ:124–132

#let first = true
#for ch in data.chapters {
  let secs = ...
  if secs.len() > 0 {
    chapter-head(ch.title, first)
    first = false   // ← Typst let 绑定不可重新赋值！
Typst 的 let 创建不可变绑定，first = false 在 Typst 中实际上是在块作用域内创建新绑定而不会修改外层变量。在 Typst 0.11+ 中这可能触发警告，或者 chapter-head 永远收到 first=true，导致每章前都 pagebreak(weak: true)（不过这里 first=true 时不 pagebreak，first=false 时才 pagebreak，实际效果可能是第一章也被 pagebreak——需要实测）。

同样的问题存在于 l2.typ:111、qa.typ:118、c.typ:147。

修复：使用计数器替代布尔标志：

#for (i, ch) in data.chapters.enumerate() {
  chapter-head(ch.title, i == 0)
